# Skills · G · part 70

940 entries, gnps-library-search-results-retrieval to go-commit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **gnps-library-search-results-retrieval**: Use when the goal is a repository-scale chemical annotation table across public tandem MS files, e.g. reproducing the ReDU compound-by-file GNPS library search matrix or updating ReDU's identification database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-search-results-retrieval))
- **gnps-library-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when preparing a downloaded GNPS MGF library (e.g. ALL_GNPS.mgf) as training data or as a cleaned search library for MS2Deepscore/Spec2Vec/MS2Query, so that spectra are uniform and noise-free. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-spectrum-preprocessing))
- **gnps-library-spectrum-processing**: Use when a public GNPS/MassBank/MoNA .msp library needs reproducible cleaning before library matching or machine-learning training: metadata harmonization, adduct/parent-mass repair, annotation validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-library-spectrum-processing))
- **gnps-massive-dataset-execution**: Use when a GC-MS dataset deposited on GNPS/MassIVE (an MSV accession) must be processed into deconvoluted compound fragmentation spectra via the MSHub/GNPS workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-massive-dataset-execution))
- **gnps-massive-dataset-retrieval-and-reprocessing**: Use when reproducing or extending analyses from a publicly deposited untargeted metabolomics dataset (e.g. peak-area feature tables plus sample metadata hosted on GNPS/MassIVE or Qiita) instead of acquiring new instrument data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-massive-dataset-retrieval-and-reprocessing))
- **gnps-masst-repository-mining**: Use when KGMN (or similar) has produced putatively annotated unknown metabolites with precursor m/z and MS/MS spectra and you need to test whether they recur in public metabolomics repositories (GNPS/MassIVE) as an orthogonal validation signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-masst-repository-mining))
- **gnps-matching-peak-scoring**: Use when dereplicating preprocessed MS2 .mzML query spectra against GNPS, HMDB, and MassBank spectral libraries and scoring candidate metabolite matches per LC-MS2 feature. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-matching-peak-scoring))
- **gnps-metadata-sample-mapping**: Use when running Inventa or GNPS Feature-Based Molecular Networking on an extract set and you must supply a metadata table with cleaned, up-to-date species names and organ information per sample. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-metadata-sample-mapping))
- **gnps-metadata-schema-validation**: Use when preparing to submit a dataset (e.g. MSV000083437) to MassIVE or launch a GNPS molecular networking job and the metadata table must be checked/normalized to the GNPS sample-information schema. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-metadata-schema-validation))
- **gnps-metadata-taxonomy-column-mapping**: Use when building the Inventa priority rank from a GNPS FBMN job and the metadata table must carry cleaned species/genus/family columns so LC can be computed from LOTUS taxonomic compound counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-metadata-taxonomy-column-mapping))
- **gnps-mgf-format-handling**: Use when you have mass spectrometry MS/MS spectral data in GNPS-style MGF format and need to feed it into the Mass2SMILES deep learning model for structure and functional group prediction. - **gnps-modified-cosine-fingerprint-computation**: Use when query MS/MS spectra (e.g., MassQL-filtered siderophore hits) must be converted into sample-wise similarity fingerprints to feed the NPOmix k-nearest neighbor classifier or compare spectra across paired genome–metabolome samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/gnps-modified-cosine-fingerprint-computation))
- **gnps-modified-cosine-scoring**: Use when building MS/MS testing-matrix fingerprints for NPOmix: each query spectrum needs a maximum modified cosine score against experimental spectra of each genome-matched sample. - **gnps-molecular-family-mapping**: Use when a metabolomics intensity matrix contains peaks grouped by tandem-MS fragmentation spectra (GNPS FBMN Molecular Families, MS2LDA Mass2Motifs) and these groupings must be turned into metabolite-set membership tables for PALS activity-level scoring. - **gnps-molecular-network-integration**: Use when you have computed frequent fragmentation patterns from a collection of MS/MS spectra using mineMS2, and you want to focus pattern interpretation on subsets of spectra that form meaningful network components (connected groups, cliques, or high-similarity pairs) in a GNPS molecular network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/gnps-molecular-network-integration) · 3 copies)
- **gnps-molecular-network-interpretation**: Use when you have a GNPS molecular network with detected components (e.g. pairs or clusters of similar MS/MS spectra) and a library of mineMS2 fragmentation patterns, and you want the top patterns explaining each component. - **gnps-molecular-networking-annotation**: Use when you have an MGF file of tandem MS spectra plus a MZmine (&gt;2.53) quantitative feature table and need per-node spectral-library annotation scores (cosine scores) for every ion feature before k-aggregation. - **gnss-carrier-smoothing**: Use when you must smooth GNSS code pseudoranges with carrier-phase delta ranges before positioning: run the first-order Hatch recursion at a smoothing time constant, carry the smoothed range between epochs on the precise carrier increments, and monitor the code-carrier ionospheric divergence whose trailing-window slope fit predicts the smoothed-minus-code bias that would alarm a diverging range. Computes the noise-reduction verdict from the exact steady-state code-noise closed form sigma_code*sqrt(alpha/(2 - alpha)), its sigma_code/sqrt(2*tau/T) textbook limit and the carrier delta-range term… ([ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills](https://github.com/ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gnc-autonomy/navigation/gnss-carrier-smoothing) · ★8)
- **gnss-doppler-velocity-positioning**: Use when you must estimate the 3-D velocity of a GNSS receiver and its receiver clock drift at a single epoch from carrier-phase delta-range-rate (doppler) observables: propagate each satellite ECEF position and velocity from the broadcast-ephemeris Kepler elements, form the line-of-sight unit vector from the receiver position (pseudorange fix first, or supplied), predict the range rate rho_dot = (v_sat - v_rec) dot u + c*dt_dot, and solve the iterated least-squares system over the per-satellite doppler residuals, rows [u, -1.0], by 4x4 normal equations for the receiver velocity and clock dri… ([ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills](https://github.com/ashfordeOU/aero-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gnc-autonomy/navigation/gnss-doppler-velocity-positioning) · ★8)

- **gnss-navigation-knowledge-patch**: GNSS ([Nevaberry/nevaberry-plugins](https://github.com/Nevaberry/nevaberry-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/knowledge-patch/patches-claude/gnss-navigation-knowledge-patch) · ★25 · 2 copies)
- **gnss-pseudorange-positioning**: Use when you must compute a GNSS receiver position fix from pseudorange measurements: given satellite positions in ECEF and their pseudoranges (geometric range plus receiver clock bias), solve the four-unknown navigation equations for x, y, z and clock bias with an iterated least-squares adjustment and 4x4 normal equation solves.
